# Phase 5 benchmark gate

## Completed measurement summary

All 42 OS/runtime legs completed: three full v5 gates, three full same-job phase1 comparisons, and six Shared legs. Exact production candidate/baseline identities, stable binaries, actual runtimes, all rounds and nonnegative counters validated. This is a completed measurement task; the release gate is **not qualified** because failures and inconclusive results remain.

### Shared report-only ratios

Default one-file candidate vs v5 Shared, one uncontended process. Six Linux/Windows rounds, ten macOS. Open/close includes collection-name validation; write excludes the separately timed single commit of 1,000 documents. The very large open/close cost is visible and is not waived by faster reads. v6 durable settings and v5 do not promise identical per-commit device durability.

|OS/runtime|Config|Open/close|Read|Write in transaction|Commit|

|Linux net8.0|dev-os-cache|71.8061|0.1723|1.0882|0.4019|

### Final gated failure list

|OS/runtime|Scenario|Failing job median ratios|
|---|---|---:|
|Linux net10.0|bulk_insert_100k|1.1638, 1.1668, 1.1222|
|Linux net8.0|bulk_insert_100k|1.2036, 1.1714|
|Windows net10.0|bulk_insert_100k|1.2008, 1.1809|
|Windows net8.0|bulk_insert_100k|1.1564|

8 failing gated rows across all six runs; 65 noisy/inconclusive rows. All confirmed gated failures are bulk (#3145).
Repeated phase1-envelope shifts and the Shared open cost are additional performance findings; report-only status does not erase them. No product fix or threshold change was made.

---


Candidate `e507faad40e968aa2fcf63ae6dd01fd7def2e07b`; phase1 `a8727ffce5d11d8fb74a87c3dd6d2ce8e3d7def3`.
Measurement only. v5 is NuGet 5.0.21. Production Release, TestingEnabled=false, identical warmed sources, TieredCompilation=0, pre-timer GC normalization. Six rounds per Linux/Windows job, ten macOS. Each repetition is reported separately; host/runtime results are never pooled. Bounds are observed candidate min / baseline max and candidate max / baseline min, not confidence intervals.

Single insert is report-only per #3134. Durable default and writer scaling are separate; v5 has no per-commit device-sync setting. Bulk remains gated at 1.10x (#3145); point lookup 1.05x; other gated scenarios 1.10x.

A/A reference: [42360152589](https://github.com/litedb-org/LiteDB/actions/runs/42360152589), from p1-cpu-gcharness.md. Every phase4/phase1 median above its matching scenario/config/OS/runtime A/A upper bound (floored at 1) is flagged; this historical envelope is diagnostic, not a statistical confidence interval. No new acceptance thresholds.
